<h1 dir="RTL" style="text-align: justify;"><strong>גאורג פרידריך הנדל</strong></h1>
<div style="text-align: justify;"><span style="font-size: 14px;"><strong>גאורג פרידריך הנדל (ב<a title="גרמנית" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%92%D7%A8%D7%9E%D7%A0%D7%99%D7%AA">גרמנית</a>: <span dir="LTR">George Frideric</span> <span dir="LTR">Handel</span>;‏ <a title="23 בפברואר" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/23_%D7%91%D7%A4%D7%91%D7%A8%D7%95%D7%90%D7%A8">23 בפברואר</a> <a title="1685" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1685">1685</a> – <a title="14 באפריל" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/14_%D7%91%D7%90%D7%A4%D7%A8%D7%99%D7%9C">14 באפריל</a> <a title="1759" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1759">1759</a>) היה <a title="מלחין" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%9E%D7%9C%D7%97%D7%99%D7%9F">מלחין</a> בן תקופת ה<a title="מוזיקת בארוק" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%9E%D7%95%D7%96%D7%99%D7%A7%D7%AA_%D7%91%D7%90%D7%A8%D7%95%D7%A7">בארוק</a>. יצירתו המפורסמת ביותר היא <a title="משיח (אורטוריה)" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%9E%D7%A9%D7%99%D7%97_(%D7%90%D7%95%D7%A8%D7%98%D7%95%D7%A8%D7%99%D7%94)">משיח</a> – <u><a title="אורטוריה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%90%D7%95%D7%A8%D7%98%D7%95%D7%A8%D7%99%D7%94">אורטוריה</a></u> לסולנים, <a title="מקהלה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%9E%D7%A7%D7%94%D7%9C%D7%94">מקהלה</a> ו<a title="תזמורת" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%AA%D7%96%D7%9E%D7%95%D7%A8%D7%AA">תזמורת</a>, המושרת ב<a title="חג המולד" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%97%D7%92_%D7%94%D7%9E%D7%95%D7%9C%D7%93">חג המולד</a> או בחג ה<a title="פסחא" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%A4%D7%A1%D7%97%D7%90">פסחא</a>.<br>הנדל נולד ב<a title="האלה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%94%D7%90%D7%9C%D7%94">האלה</a> שב<a title="סקסוניה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%A1%D7%A7%D7%A1%D7%95%D7%A0%D7%99%D7%94">סקסוניה</a> ב-1685, לאב שהיה <a title="ספר (מקצוע)" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%A1%D7%A4%D7%A8_(%D7%9E%D7%A7%D7%A6%D7%95%D7%A2)">ספר</a> ו<a title="חובש" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%97%D7%95%D7%91%D7%A9">חובש</a>. כבר בגיל שבע התגלה כ<a title="פסנתר" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%A4%D7%A1%D7%A0%D7%AA%D7%A8">פסנתרן</a> ונגן <a title="עוגב" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%A2%D7%95%D7%92%D7%91">עוגב</a> מוכשר, ובגיל תשע החל להלחין. החל ב-<a title="1700" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1700">1700</a> למד אצל <a title="פרידריך וילהלם צאכו (הדף אינו קיים)" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=%D7%A4%D7%A8%D7%99%D7%93%D7%A8%D7%99%D7%9A_%D7%95%D7%99%D7%9C%D7%94%D7%9C%D7%9D_%D7%A6%D7%90%D7%9B%D7%95&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1">פרידריך וילהלם צאכו</a> (<span dir="LTR">Zachow</span>), נגן העוגב של כנסיית הגבירה (<span dir="LTR">Liebfrauenkirche</span>). בשנת <a title="1702" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1702">1702</a> החל בלימודי <a title="משפטים" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%9E%D7%A9%D7%A4%D7%98%D7%99%D7%9D">משפטים</a> ב<a title="אוניברסיטת האלה (הדף אינו קיים)" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=%D7%90%D7%95%D7%A0%D7%99%D7%91%D7%A8%D7%A1%D7%99%D7%98%D7%AA_%D7%94%D7%90%D7%9C%D7%94&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1">אוניברסיטת האלה</a> במצוות אביו. כעבור שנה פרש מלימודיו לטובת ה<a title="מוזיקה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%9E%D7%95%D7%96%D7%99%D7%A7%D7%94">מוזיקה</a>, וזכה במשרת <a title="כינור" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%9B%D7%99%D7%A0%D7%95%D7%A8">כנר</a> בתזמורת בית ה<a title="אופרה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%90%D7%95%D7%A4%D7%A8%D7%94">אופרה</a> של <a title="המבורג" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%94%D7%9E%D7%91%D7%95%D7%A8%D7%92">המבורג</a>. שם יצר בשנת <a title="1705" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1705">1705</a> את האופרות הראשונות שלו, "אלמירה" ו"נרו", ובשנת <a title="1708" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1708">1708</a> שתי אופרות מוקדמות נוספות, "דפני" ו"פלורינדו". בשנים <a title="1706" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1706">1706</a>–<a title="1709" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1709">1709</a> יצא למסעות שונים, ולמד ב<a title="איטליה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%90%D7%99%D7%98%D7%9C%D7%99%D7%94">איטליה</a>. ב<a title="פירנצה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%A4%D7%99%D7%A8%D7%A0%D7%A6%D7%94">פירנצה</a> הלחין ב-1707 את "רודריגו", וב<a title="ונציה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%95%D7%A0%D7%A6%D7%99%D7%94">וונציה</a> הלחין ב-1708 את "אגריפינה". ב<a title="רומא" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%A8%D7%95%D7%9E%D7%90">רומא</a> כתב ב-<a title="1710" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1710">1710</a> שתי אורטוריות.<br>בשנת 1710 היה למוזיקאי החצר של הנסיך גאורג מ<a title="הנובר" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%94%D7%A0%D7%95%D7%91%D7%A8">הנובר</a>, ועם המלכתו של גאורג לודוויג ל<a title="ג'ורג' הראשון, מלך בריטניה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%92%27%D7%95%D7%A8%D7%92%27_%D7%94%D7%A8%D7%90%D7%A9%D7%95%D7%9F,_%D7%9E%D7%9C%D7%9A_%D7%91%D7%A8%D7%99%D7%98%D7%A0%D7%99%D7%94">ג'ורג' הראשון</a> <a title="מלכי בריטניה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%9E%D7%9C%D7%9B%D7%99_%D7%91%D7%A8%D7%99%D7%98%D7%A0%D7%99%D7%94">מלך</a> <a title="ממלכת בריטניה הגדולה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%9E%D7%9E%D7%9C%D7%9B%D7%AA_%D7%91%D7%A8%D7%99%D7%98%D7%A0%D7%99%D7%94_%D7%94%D7%92%D7%93%D7%95%D7%9C%D7%94">בריטניה הגדולה</a> בשנת <a title="1714" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1714">1714</a>, הגיע לחצר המלוכה האנגלית והיה למוזיקאי החצר שלה. שכרו בסך 200 <a title="לירה שטרלינג" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%9C%D7%99%D7%A8%D7%94_%D7%A9%D7%98%D7%A8%D7%9C%D7%99%D7%A0%D7%92">לירות שטרלינג</a> שולם בידי המלכה אן (בשנת 1713 כתב לכבודה <a title="אודה ליום הולדתה של המלכה אנה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%90%D7%95%D7%93%D7%94_%D7%9C%D7%99%D7%95%D7%9D_%D7%94%D7%95%D7%9C%D7%93%D7%AA%D7%94_%D7%A9%D7%9C_%D7%94%D7%9E%D7%9C%D7%9B%D7%94_%D7%90%D7%A0%D7%94">אודה</a>). בשנת <a title="1719" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1719">1719</a> ייסד את ה<a title="האקדמיה המלכותית למוזיקה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%94%D7%90%D7%A7%D7%93%D7%9E%D7%99%D7%94_%D7%94%D7%9E%D7%9C%D7%9B%D7%95%D7%AA%D7%99%D7%AA_%D7%9C%D7%9E%D7%95%D7%96%D7%99%D7%A7%D7%94">אקדמיה המלכותית למוזיקה</a>, ובראשה עמד בשנים <a title="1720" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1720">1720</a>–<a title="1728" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1728">1728</a>. בשנים <a title="1729" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1729">1729</a>–<a title="1734" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1734">1734</a> היה שותף בניהול ה<a title="תיאטרון" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%AA%D7%99%D7%90%D7%98%D7%A8%D7%95%D7%9F">תיאטרון</a> המלכותי. החל ב-<a title="1732" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1732">1732</a> כתב בעיקר אורטוריות.<br>בשנת <a title="1737" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1737">1737</a> חלה ב<a title="שיתוק" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%A9%D7%99%D7%AA%D7%95%D7%A7">שיתוק</a> בעקבות <a title="התקף לב" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%94%D7%AA%D7%A7%D7%A3_%D7%9C%D7%91">התקף לב</a>, אבל נרפא אחרי שנסע ל<a title="אאכן" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%90%D7%90%D7%9B%D7%9F">אאכן</a>. בשנת <a title="1751" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1751">1751</a> <a title="עיוורון" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%A2%D7%99%D7%95%D7%95%D7%A8%D7%95%D7%9F">התעוור</a>, וכעבור שמונה שנים נוספות מת ב<a title="לונדון" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%9C%D7%95%D7%A0%D7%93%D7%95%D7%9F">לונדון</a>. הוא נקבר ב<a title="כנסיית וסטמינסטר" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%9B%D7%A0%D7%A1%D7%99%D7%99%D7%AA_%D7%95%D7%A1%D7%98%D7%9E%D7%99%D7%A0%D7%A1%D7%98%D7%A8">כנסיית וסטמינסטר</a>.<br>יצירתו של הנדל מקיפה כחמישים <a title="אופרה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%90%D7%95%D7%A4%D7%A8%D7%94">אופרות</a>, שלושים ושתיים <a title="אורטוריה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%90%D7%95%D7%A8%D7%98%D7%95%D7%A8%D7%99%D7%94">אורטוריות</a>, אוסף גדול של <a title="מוזיקה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%9E%D7%95%D7%96%D7%99%D7%A7%D7%94">מוזיקה</a> <a title="כנסייה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%9B%D7%A0%D7%A1%D7%99%D7%99%D7%94">כנסייתית</a> ואוסף גדול אף יותר של <a title="קטע כליי" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%A7%D7%98%D7%A2_%D7%9B%D7%9C%D7%99%D7%99">מוזיקה כלית</a>.<br><br>מיודענו הנדל תרם תרומה מכרעת בעקיפין להקמתו של העם היהודי בארץ ישראל. וזאת בזכות שר החוץ הבריטי דאז בלפור (הצהרת בלפור 2/11/2017 להקמתו של העם היהודי בארץ ישראל יחד עם בנימין זאב הרצל ) בלפור אהב את המוזיקה של הנדל בלפור היה ספוג הנדל ושמע רבות מיצירותיו... שמענו את <span style="color: #0000ff;">הקונצ'רטו גרוסו ברה מינור</span> אשר הלחין הנדל, כאן אנו נחשפים למוזיקה המייחדת כל כך את הנדל <u>הפתיחה הצרפתית</u>&nbsp;אשר מכילה מוזיקה איטית בשילוב הקצב המנוקד צליל ארוך שמייד לאחריו צליל קצר מה שמכנים קצב מנוקד..<br>מדובר במוזיקה פוליפונית בשילוב <span style="color: #0000cd;"><u>פוגה </u></span></strong></span><span dir="RTL"><span style="font-size: 14px;"><strong>- יצירה הכתובה בשיטת הקונטרפונקט בשני קולות או יותר (בדרך כלל 3 או 4 קולות) המטפלת בנושא קצר על פי כללים קפדניים. הפוגה מבוססת על נושא, שתחילה מציג אותו קול אחד לבדו ואחרי כמה תיבות או מספר צלילים הקולות האחרים נכנסים בזה אחר זה בחיקוי, עד שכל הקולות נשמעים בעת אחת. ההצגה הראשונה של הנושא היא בטוניקה ונקראת דוקס והשנייה (בקול הנכנס השני) היא בגובה שונה מעט בדרך כלל בדומיננטה ונקראת קומס . הקול השלישי חוזר לטוניקה והרביעי שוב בדומיננטה, עד שכל הקולות נכנסים ותמה התצוגה הראשונה של הנושא. בכל קול מתמלא הרווח בין הצגה להצגה של הנושא בקונטרפונקט חופשי מחומר המבוסס על הנושא. אחרי התצוגה הראשונה מתחיל חלק המעבר המבוסס על מוטיבים מן הנושא או מנושא נגדי, ויש בו מודולציות, מעברים מסולם לסולם כמו למינור המקביל, לדומיננטה או לסוב דומיננטה. המבנה הכללי של הפוגה הוא חילופין של תצוגות ומעברים, או אפיזודות שבסופן חזרה לטוניקה בתצוגה האחרונה שאחריה לעתים קודה על נקודת עוגב . אפשר להלחין פוגות כמעט לכל הרכב כלי או קולי, פוגה עשויה להיות יצירה עצמאית או חלק מיצירה גדולה כמו סונטה, סומפוניה או אופרה. אף על פי שהפוגה נחשבת לצורה בארוקית, לא מעטים המלחינים המאוחרים יותר שהשתמשו בה. למשל הנדל, מוצרט, בטהובן, ברהמס. ובמאה ה-20 ע"י סטרוינסקי, שוסטקוביץ ועוד..&nbsp;</strong></span></span><strong><span style="font-size: 14px;">&nbsp;</span><br><br><span style="font-size: 14px;">האורטוריות המוכרות שלו הן:</span></strong></div>
<ul>
<li dir="RTL" style="text-align: justify;"><span style="font-size: 14px;"><strong>אסתר (1720)</strong></span></li>
<li dir="RTL" style="text-align: justify;"><span style="font-size: 14px;"><strong>צדוק הכהן (1717), משמשת בטקסי הכתרת מלכים וכ<a title="המנון ליגת האלופות" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%94%D7%9E%D7%A0%D7%95%D7%9F_%D7%9C%D7%99%D7%92%D7%AA_%D7%94%D7%90%D7%9C%D7%95%D7%A4%D7%95%D7%AA">המנון ליגת האלופות</a></strong></span></li>
<li dir="RTL" style="text-align: justify;"><span style="font-size: 14px;"><strong>שאול (<a title="1738" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1738">1738</a>, בכורה ב-<a title="1739" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1739">1739</a>)</strong></span></li>
<li dir="RTL" style="text-align: justify;"><span style="font-size: 14px;"><strong>ישראל במצרים (1739)</strong></span></li>
<li dir="RTL" style="text-align: justify;"><span style="font-size: 14px;"><strong>והמפורסמת מכולן: <a title="משיח (אורטוריה)" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%9E%D7%A9%D7%99%D7%97_(%D7%90%D7%95%D7%A8%D7%98%D7%95%D7%A8%D7%99%D7%94)">משיח</a> (<a title="1741" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1741">1741</a>)</strong></span></li>
<li dir="RTL" style="text-align: justify;"><span style="font-size: 14px;"><strong>שמשון (<a title="1743" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1743">1743</a>)</strong></span></li>
<li dir="RTL" style="text-align: justify;"><span style="font-size: 14px;"><strong>בלשצר (1743)</strong></span></li>
<li dir="RTL" style="text-align: justify;"><span style="font-size: 14px;"><strong>יהודה המכבי (<a title="1745" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/1745">1745</a>) (מתוכה נלקח הלחן לשיר ה<a title="חנוכה" href="http://he.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D7%97%D7%A0%D7%95%D7%9B%D7%94">חנוכה</a> "הבה נרימה" -&nbsp;!<span dir="LTR">HWV 63 - See the conqu'ring hero comes</span>)</strong></span></li>

<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 3.85pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">דַּבְּרוּ עַל-לֵב יְרוּשָׁלִַם, וְקִרְאוּ אֵלֶיהָ -</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 3.85pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">כִּי מָלְאָה צְבָאָהּ, כִּי נִרְצָה עֲו‍ֹנָהּ: </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 3.85pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">כִּי לָקְחָה מִיַּד ה', </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 3.85pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">כִּפְלַיִם בְּכָל-חַטֹּאתֶיהָ. </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 3.85pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">קוֹל קוֹרֵא-בַּמִּדְבָּר,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 3.85pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>פַּנּוּ דֶּרֶךְ ה'; יַשְּׁרוּ,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 3.85pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>בָּעֲרָבָה, מְסִלָּה, לֵאלֹוהֵינוּ</span><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: right; mso-outline-level: 1; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיה פרק מ' פסוקים א'- ג'</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: right; mso-outline-level: 1; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right">&nbsp;</p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: right; mso-outline-level: 1; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right">&nbsp;</p>

<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">Air - Tenor</span></strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';"> </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: justify; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Every valley shall be exalted, and every mountain and hill made low; the crooked straight and the rough places plain.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 1; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Isaiah 40: 4</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 1.7pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">אריה<span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span>- טנור</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">כָּל-גֶּיא, ייִנָּשֵׂא, וְכָל-הַר וְגִבְעָה, יִשְׁפָּלוּ;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">וְהָיָה הֶעָקֹב לְמִישׁוֹר,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">וְהָרְכָסִים לְבִקְעָה</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: right; mso-outline-level: 1; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיה פרק מ' פסוק ד'</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 4;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">4. Chorus</span></strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">And the glory of the Lord</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">shall be revealed,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">and all flesh shall see it together:</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">for the mouth of the Lord hath spoken it.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Isaiah 40: 5</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">. מקהלה</span></strong><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">4</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">וְנִגְלָה, כְּבוֹד ה';</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm -5.4pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>וְרָאוּ כָל-בָּשָׂר יַחְדָּו, </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>כִּי פִּי ה' דִּבֵּר</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיהו פרק מ' פסוק ה</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 1.7pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 5;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">5. Accompagnato-</span></strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;"> Bass</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Thus saith the Lord, the Lord of hosts: Yet once a little while and I will shake the heavens and the earth, the sea and the dry land.<br>And I will shake all nations; and the desire of all nations shall come.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Haggai 2: 6,7</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="font-size: 13.5pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #00b0f0;">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">5. רצ'יטטיב מלווה- בס</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 1.7pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">כִּי כֹה אָמַר ה' צְבָאוֹת, </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 1.7pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">עוֹד אַחַת מְעַט הִיא;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 1.7pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>וַאֲנִי, מַרְעִישׁ אֶת-הַשָּׁמַיִם וְאֶת-הָאָרֶץ,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 1.7pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>וְאֶת-הַיָּם,וְאֶת-הֶחָרָבָה</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 1.7pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">חגי פרק ב' פסוקים ו'-ז'</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 6;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">The Lord, whom ye seek, shall suddenly come to His temple, even the messenger of the Covenant, whom you delight in; behold, He shall come, saith the Lord of hosts.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">(</span></strong><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Malachi 3: 1)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="font-size: 13.5pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #00b0f0;">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">וּפִתְאֹם יָבוֹא אֶל-הֵיכָלוֹ הָאָדוֹן אֲשֶׁר-אַתֶּם מְבַקְשִׁים, וּמַלְאַךְ הַבְּרִית אֲשֶׁר-אַתֶּם חֲפֵצִים הִנֵּה-בָא-אָמַר, ה' צְבָאוֹת</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">מלאכי פרק ג' פסוק א</span></strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">,</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 7;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">6. Air- Alto or soprano </span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">But who may abide the day of His coming, and who shall stand when He ap pear eth? For He is like a refiner's fire.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span><strong>(Malachi 3: 2)</strong></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">6. אריה-<span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span>אלט או סופרן</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 1.7pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">וּמִי מְכַלְכֵּל אֶת-יוֹם בּוֹאוֹ,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 1.7pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">וּמִי הָעֹמֵד בְּהֵרָאוֹתוֹ: </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 1.7pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">כִּי-הוּא כְּאֵשׁ מְצָרֵף, וּכְבֹרִית מְכַבְּסִים.</span><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">מלאכי : פרק 3 פסוק ב'</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 8;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">7. Chorus</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">And He shall purify the sons of Levi , that they may offer unto the Lord an offering in righteousness.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Malachi 3: 3)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">7. מקהלה</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">וְטִהַר אֶת-בְּנֵי-לֵוִי וְזִקַּק אֹתָם,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">כַּזָּהָב </span><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">וְכַכָּסֶף; וְהָיוּ, לַ-ה', </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">מַגִּישֵׁי מִנְחָה, בִּצְדָקָה</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">מלאכי : פרק 3 פסוק ג'</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 14.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 9;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">8. Recitative-Alto</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Behold, a virgin shall conceive and bear a son, and shall call His name Emmanuel, God with us.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Isaiah 7: 14</span></strong><strong><span style="font-size: 13.5pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 13.5pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">. רצ'יטטיב- אלט</span></strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;"> </span><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">8</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">הִנֵּה הָעַלְמָה, הָרָה וְיֹלֶדֶת בֵּן,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">וְקָרָאת שְׁמוֹ, עִמָּנוּ אֵל</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיהו : פרק ז פסוק יד'</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 10;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">9. Air and Chorus-Alto</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>O thou that tell est good tidings to Zion,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>get thee up into the high mountain.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>O thou that tell est good tidings to<span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span>Jerusalem, </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">lift up thy voice with strength; lift it up,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>be not afraid; say unto the cities of Judah,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>behold your god!<span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Isaiah 40:9</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">. אריה – אלט ומקהלה </span></strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">9</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">עַל הַר-גָּבֹהַּ עֲלִי-לָךְ, מְבַשֶּׂרֶת צִיּוֹן, </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">הָרִימִי בַכֹּחַ קוֹלֵךְ, מְבַשֶּׂרֶת יְרוּשָׁלִָם; </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">הָרִימִי, אַל-תִּירָאִי, אִמְרִי לְעָרֵי יְהוּדָה, </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">הִנֵּה אֱלֹהֵיכֶם</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span style="font-size: 14.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span style="font-size: 14.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיה : פרק מ פסוק ט'</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 14.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 11;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Arise, shine, for thy light is come,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>and the glory of the Lord is risen upon thee.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Isaiah 60: 1)</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">קוּמִי אוֹרִי, כִּי בָא אוֹרֵךְ;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">וּכְבוֹד יְהוָה, עָלַיִךְ זָרָח.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיה : פרק ס פסוק א'</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 12;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">9. Chorus</span></strong><span style="font-size: 13.5pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;"><br></span><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">O thou that tellest good tidings to Zion, </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">get thee up into the high mountain.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>O thou that tellest good tidings to Jerusalem,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>lift up thy voice with strength; lift it up, be not afraid; say unto the cities of Judah,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">behold your god!</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Isaiah 40:</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">9. מקהלה</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>עַל הַר-גָּבֹוהַּ עֲלִי-לָךְ, מְבַשֶּׂרֶת צִיּוֹן,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>הָרִימִי בַכֹּוחַ קוֹלֵךְ, מְבַשֶּׂרֶת יְרוּשָׁלִָם; הָרִימִי, </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>אַל-תִּירָאִי, אִמְרִי לְעָרֵי יְהוּדָה,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">הִנֵּה אֱלֹוהֵיכֶם.</span><span lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;"> </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000; mso-font-kerning: 18.0pt;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000; mso-font-kerning: 18.0pt;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000; mso-font-kerning: 18.0pt;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיה : פרק מ פסוק ט</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000; mso-font-kerning: 18.0pt;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span></span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 13;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">10. Accompagnato-Bass</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">For behold, darkness shall cover the earth, and gross darkness the people; but the Lord shall arise upon thee, and His glory shall be seen upon thee.<br>And the Gentiles shall come to thy light, and kings to the brightness of thy rising.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Isaiah 60: 2-3</span></strong></p>

</td>

<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; tab-stops: right 185.95pt 187.65pt; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 1.7pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">וַעֲרָפֶל לְאֻמִּים; וְעָלַיִךְ יִזְרַח ה',</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; tab-stops: right 185.95pt 187.65pt; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 1.7pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">וּכְבוֹדוֹ עָלַיִךְ יֵרָאֶה.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; tab-stops: right 185.95pt 187.65pt; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 1.7pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">וְהָלְכוּ גוֹיִם, לְאוֹרֵךְ;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; tab-stops: right 185.95pt 187.65pt; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 1.7pt 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">וּמְלָכִים, לְנֹגַהּ זַרְחֵךְ</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיה : פרק ס פסוקים ב',ג'</span></strong></p>
</td>

<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">11. Air-Bass</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">The people that walked in darkness</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>have seen a great light;<br>and they that dwell in the land of the shadow of death, upon them hath the light shined.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Isaiah 9:</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">11. אריה- בס</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">הָעָם הַהֹלְכִים בַּחֹשֶׁךְ, רָאוּ אוֹר גָּדוֹל:</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">יֹשְׁבֵי בְּאֶרֶץ צַלְמָוֶת, אוֹר נָגַהּ עֲלֵיהֶם.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="margin-bottom: 1.2pt; text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיה : פרק ט פסוקים א'</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 15;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">12. Chorus</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">For unto us a child is born, unto us a son is given, and the government shall be upon His shoulder; and His name shall be called Wonderful, Counsellor, the mighty God, the Everlasting Father, the Prince of Peace.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Isaiah 9: 6)</span></strong></p>

<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">12. מקהלה </span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">כִּי-יֶלֶד יֻלַּד-לָנוּ, בֵּן נִתַּן-לָנוּ,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">וַתְּהִי הַמִּשְׂרָה, עַל-שִׁכְמוֹ;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">וַיִּקְרָא שְׁמוֹ פֶּלֶא יוֹעֵץ,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">אֵל גִּבּוֹר, אֲבִי-עַד, </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">שַׂר-שָׁלוֹם.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיה : פרק ט פסוקים ו'</span></strong></p>

<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 14.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>

</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">

<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 14.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
