<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">14. Accompagnato-</span></strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">Soprano</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">And<span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span>lo , the angel of the Lord came upon them, and the glory of the Lord shone round about them, and they were sore afraid.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Luke 2: 9)</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">14. רצ'טטיב מלווה- סופרן</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">בְּאוֹתוֹ חֶבֶל אֶרֶץ הָיוּ רוֹעִים שֶׁנָּהֲגוּ לָגוּר בַּשָֹדֶה וְלִשְׁמֹר עַל עֶדְרָם בְּאַשְׁמוּרוֹת הַלַּיְלָה</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 14.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">הבשורה על פי לוקאס</span></strong><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span></span></strong><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">2:9</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 18;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">15. Recitative- Soprano</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">And the angel said unto them: "Fear not, for behold, I bring you good tidings of great joy, which shall be to all people.<br>For unto you is born this day in the city of David a Saviour, which is Christ the Lord."</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Luke 2: 10-11</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="font-size: 13.5pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">15. רצ'טטיב- סופרן</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">לְפֶתַע נִצַּב עֲלֵיהֶם מַלְאַךְ ה' וּכְבוֹד ה' נָגַהּ סְבִיבָם. הֵם נִבְהֲלוּ עַד מְאֹד</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">אוּלָם הַמַּלְאָךְ אָמַר לָהֶם: "אַל תִּפְחֲדוּ, כִּי הִנְנִי מְבַשֵֹר לָכֶם שִׂמְחָה גְּדוֹלָה אֲשֶׁר תִּהְיֶה לְכָל הָעָם. הַיּוֹם נוֹלַד לָכֶם מוֹשִׁיעַ בְּעִיר דָּוִד, הוּא הַמָּשִׁיחַ הָאָדוֹן.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">הבשורה על פי לוקאס2<span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span>-<span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span>12: 10<span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span></span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 19;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">16. Accompagnato- Soprano</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">And suddenly there was with the angel, a multitude of the heavenly host, praising God, and saying</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 13.5pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Luke 2: 13</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">16. רצ'טטיב מלווה- סופרן</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">פִּתְאוֹם הָיוּ לְיַד הַמַּלְאָךְ הֲמוֹן צְבָא הַשָּׁמַיִם וְהֵמָּה מְהַלְלִים אֶת הָאֱלֹהִים וְאוֹמְרִים</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">הבשורה על פי לוקאס<span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span>2:13<span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span></span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 20;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">17. Chorus</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">"Glory to God in the highest, and peace on earth, good will towards men."</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Luke 2: 14</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="font-size: 13.5pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">17. מקהלה</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">"כָּבוֹד לֵאלֹהִים בַּמְּרוֹמִים, וּבָאָרֶץ שָׁלוֹם עַל בְּנֵי אָדָם אֲשֶׁר אוֹתָם רָצָה</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">הבשורה על פי לוקאס<span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span>2:<span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span>14</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 14.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 21;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">18. Air- Soprano</span></strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;"> </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Rejoice greatly, O daughter of Zion; shout, O daughter of Jerusalem!<br>Behold, thy King cometh unto thee; He is the righteous Saviour, and He shall speak peace unto the heathen.<br>Rejoice greatly. . .&nbsp;da capo</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Zechariah 9: 9-10</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">. ארייה- סופרן</span></strong><span style="font-size: 14.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">18</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">גִּילִי מְאֹד בַּת-צִיּוֹן, הָרִיעִי בַּת יְרוּשָׁלִַם, הִנֵּה מַלְכֵּךְ יָבוֹא לָךְ, צַדִּיק וְנוֹשָׁע הוּא; עָנִי וְרֹכֵב עַל-חֲמוֹר, וְעַל-עַיִר בֶּן-אֲתוֹנוֹת. וְהִכְרַתִּי-רֶכֶב מֵאֶפְרַיִם, וְסוּס מִירוּשָׁלִַם, וְנִכְרְתָה קֶשֶׁת מִלְחָמָה, וְדִבֶּר שָׁלוֹם לַגּוֹיִם; וּמָשְׁלוֹ מִיָּם עַד-יָם, וּמִנָּהָר עַד-אַפְסֵי-אָרֶץ</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">זכריה פרק ט פסוקים ט-י</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 14.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 22;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">19. Recitative-Alto</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Then shall the eyes of the blind be opened, and the ears of the deaf unstopped.<br>Then shall the lame man leap as a hart, and the tongue of the dumb shall sing.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Isaiah 35: 5-6)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="font-size: 13.5pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">19. רצ'טטיב- אלטו</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">אָז תִּפָּקַחְנָה, עֵינֵי עִוְרִים; וְאָזְנֵי חֵרְשִׁים, תִּפָּתַחְנָה. אָז יְדַלֵּג כָּאַיָּל פִּסֵּחַ, וְתָרֹן לְשׁוֹן אִלֵּם: כִּי-נִבְקְעוּ בַמִּדְבָּר מַיִם, וּנְחָלִים בָּעֲרָבָה</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיה : פרק כה פסוקים ה-ו'</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 23;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">20. Air (or Duet) </span></strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">(Alto &amp; soprano)</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">He shall feed His flock like a shepherd; and He shall gather the lambs with His arm, and carry them in His bosom, and gently lead those that are with young.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Isaiah 40: 11</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">. ארייה או דואט- אלט וסופרן </span></strong><span style="font-size: 14.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">20</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">כְּרֹעֶה, עֶדְרוֹ יִרְעֶה, בִּזְרֹעוֹ יְקַבֵּץ טְלָאִים, וּבְחֵיקוֹ יִשָּׂא; עָלוֹת, יְנַהֵל</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיה : פרק מ פסוקים יא</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 14.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 24;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Come unto Him, all ye that labour, come unto Him that are heavy laden, and He will give you rest.<br>Take his yoke upon you, and learn of Him, for He is meek and lowly of heart, and ye shall find rest unto your souls.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Matthew 11: 28-29)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="font-size: 13.5pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" valign="top" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">בּוֹאוּ אֵלַי כָּל הָעֲמֵלִים וְהָעֲמוּסִים וַאֲנִי אַמְצִיא לָכֶם מְנוּחָה. קְחוּ עֲלֵיכֶם אֶת עֻלִּי וְלִמְדוּ מִמֶּנִּי, כִּי עָנָו אֲנִי וּנְמוּךְ רוּחַ; תִּמְצְאוּ מַרְגּוֹעַ לְנַפְשׁוֹתֵיכֶם</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">מתי : 11: 28-29</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 14.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 25;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">

<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">His yoke is easy, and His burden is light.</span></p>

<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>

</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 26; mso-yfti-lastrow: yes;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">22. Chorus</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Behold the Lamb of <span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>God, that <span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>taketh away the sin of the world.</span></p>

<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">22. מקהלה</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black; mso-color-alt: windowtext;">הנה שה האלוהים אשר יישא את חטאת העולם כולו..</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: right; mso-outline-level: 1; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">מתוך הברית החדשה : 1: 29</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: center; mso-outline-level: 1; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="center"><strong><u><span style="font-size: 16.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black; mso-font-kerning: 18.0pt;">PART TWO</span></u></strong></p>
<div dir="ltr" align="left">
<table class="MsoNormalTable" style="width: 495.0pt; margin-left: 5.4pt; border-collapse: collapse; border: none; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-yfti-tbllook: 1184; mso-padding-alt: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt; mso-border-insideh: .5pt solid windowtext; mso-border-insidev: .5pt solid windowtext;" border="1" width="660" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0">
<tbody>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 0; mso-yfti-firstrow: yes;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">23. Air-Alto</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">He was despised and rejected of men, a man of sorrows and acquainted with grief.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Isaiah 53: 3)</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-left: none; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">23. ארייה- אלט</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">נִבְזֶה וַחֲדַל אִישִׁים, אִישׁ מַכְאֹובוֹת וִידוּעַ חֹלִי; וּכְמַסְתֵּר פָּנִים מִמֶּנּוּ, נִבְזֶה וְלֹא חֲשַׁבְנֻהוּ</span><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: right; mso-outline-level: 1; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיה : פרק נג פסוק ג</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 1;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;">He gave His back to the smiters, and His cheeks to them that plucked off His hair: He hid not His face from shame and spitting.<br>He was despised. . .&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></strong><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Isaiah 53: 6)&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">נִגַּשׂ וְהוּא נַעֲנֶה, וְלֹא יִפְתַּח-פִּיו, כַּשֶּׂה לַטֶּבַח יוּבָל, וּכְרָחֵל לִפְנֵי גֹזְזֶיהָ נֶאֱלָמָה; וְלֹא יִפְתַּח, פִּיו</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיה : פרק נג פסוק ו'</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 2;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">24. Chorus</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Surely He hath borne our griefs, and carried our sorrows!<br>He was wounded for our transgressions, He was bruised for our iniquities; the chastisement of our peace was upon Him.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Isaiah 53: 4-5)</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">24.מקהלה</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">אָכֵן חֳלָיֵנוּ הוּא נָשָׂא, וּמַכְאֹבֵינוּ סְבָלָם; וַאֲנַחְנוּ חֲשַׁבְנֻהוּ, נָגוּעַ מֻכֵּה אֱלֹהִים וּמְעֻנֶּה</span><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">.&nbsp;<span class="apple-converted-space">&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">וְהוּא מְחֹלָל מִפְּשָׁעֵנוּ, מְדֻכָּא מֵעֲו‍ֹנֹתֵינוּ; מוּסַר שְׁלוֹמֵנוּ עָלָיו</span><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; mso-ascii-font-family: Arial; mso-hansi-font-family: Arial; color: black; background: white;">, </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיה : פרק נג פסוק ד'-ה'</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 3;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">25. Chorus</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">And with His stripes we are healed.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Isaiah 53: 5)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">.מקהלה</span></strong><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">25</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">וּבַחֲבֻרָתוֹ נִרְפָּא-לָנוּ</span><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">.&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיה : פרק נג פסוק ה'</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 4;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">26. Chorus</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">All we like sheep have gone astray; we have turned every one to his own way. And the Lord hath laid on Him the iniquity of us all.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Isaiah 53: 6)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">26. מקהלה</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">כֻּלָּנוּ כַּצֹּאן תָּעִינוּ, </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">אִישׁ לְדַרְכּוֹ פָּנִינוּ; וַיהוָה הִפְגִּיעַ בּוֹ, אֵת עֲו‍ֹן כֻּלָּנו</span><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 20.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">ּ</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיה : פרק נג פסוק ו'</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 5;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">27. Accompagnato- Tenor</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">All they that see Him laugh Him to scorn; they shoot out their lips, and shake their heads, saying:</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Psalm 22: 7</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 13.5pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">. רצ'יטטיב מלווה- טנור</span></strong><span style="font-size: 14.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">27</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">וְאָנֹכִי תוֹלַעַת וְלֹא-אִישׁ; &nbsp;&nbsp; חֶרְפַּת אָדָם, וּבְזוּי עָם</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: center; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="center"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: center; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="center"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: center; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="center"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(</span></strong><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">תהלים פרק כב' פסוק ז</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 6;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">28. Chorus</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">"He trusted in God that He would deliver Him; let Him deliver Him, if He delight in Him."</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Psalm 22: 8</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">28. מקהלה</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">גֹּל אֶל-יְהוָה יְפַלְּטֵהוּ; &nbsp;&nbsp; יַצִּילֵהוּ, כִּי חָפֵץ בּוֹ</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">תהלים פרק כב' פסוק ח'</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 7;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">29. Accompagnato-Tenor</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Thy rebuke hath broken His heart: He is full of heaviness. He looked for some to have pity on Him, but there was no man, neither found He any to comfort him.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Psalm 69: 20</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">29. רצ'יטטיב מלווה- טנור</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">אַתָּה יָדַעְתָּ--חֶרְפָּתִי וּבָשְׁתִּי, וּכְלִמָּתִי; &nbsp;&nbsp; נֶגְדְּךָ, כָּל-צוֹרְרָי</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(</span></strong><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">תהלים פרק סט' פסוק כ'</span></strong><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 8;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">30. Arioso-Tenor</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Behold, and see if there be any sorrow like unto His sorrow.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Lamentations 1: 12</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">30. ארייה -טנור</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="RTL"><span lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">הביטו וראו האם יש צער כצערו .</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="RTL"><span lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">איכה פרק א פסוק יב'</span></strong><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 9;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">31. Accompagnato-Soprano or tenor</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">He was cut off out of the land of the living: for the transgressions of Thy people was He stricken.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Isaiah 53: 8)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 13.5pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 8.8pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">31. רצ'יטטיב מלווה- </span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">סופרן או טנור </span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">מֵעֹצֶר וּמִמִּשְׁפָּט לֻקָּח, וְאֶת-דּוֹרוֹ מִי יְשׂוֹחֵחַ:&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">כִּי נִגְזַר מֵאֶרֶץ חַיִּים, מִפֶּשַׁע עַמִּי נֶגַע לָמוֹ</span><span style="font-size: 20.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(</span></strong><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיה פרק נג פסוק ח</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 10;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">32. Air- Soprano or tenor</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">But Thou didst not leave His soul in hell; nor didst Thou suffer Thy Holy One to see corruption.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 13.5pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>

<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">ארייה- סופרן או טנור </span></strong><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">.32</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">כִּי, לֹא- תַעֲזֹוב נַפְשִׁי לִשְׁאוֹל; &nbsp;&nbsp; </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">לֹא- תִיתֵּן חֲסִידְךָ, לִרְאוֹת שָׁחַת</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">תהלים פרק טז' פסוק י')</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>

<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 11;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">33. Chorus</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Lift up your heads, O ye gates; and be ye lift up, ye everlasting doors; and the King of Glory shall come in.<br>Who is this King of Glory? The Lord strong and mighty, The Lord mighty in battle.<br>Lift up your heads, O ye gates; and be ye lift up, ye everlasting doors; and the King of Glory shall come in.<br>Who is this King of Glory? The Lord of Hosts, He is the King of Glory.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Psalm 24: 7-10</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">33. מקהלה </span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">שְׂאוּ שְׁעָרִים, רָאשֵׁיכֶם</span><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; mso-ascii-font-family: Arial; mso-hansi-font-family: Arial; color: black; background: white;">, &nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">וְהִנָּשְׂאוּ, פִּתְחֵי עוֹלָם</span><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black;"><br></span><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">וְיָבוֹא,<span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span>מֶלֶךְ הַכָּבוֹד</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">מִי זֶה, &nbsp;מֶלֶךְ הַכָּבוֹד</span><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black;"><br></span><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">יְהוָה, עִזּוּז וְגִבּוֹר; &nbsp;&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>יְהוָה, גִּבּוֹר מִלְחָמָה</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">שְׂאוּ שְׁעָרִים, רָאשֵׁיכֶם, &nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">&nbsp; וּשְׂאוּ, פִּתְחֵי עוֹלָם</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">וְיָבֹא, &nbsp;&nbsp; מֶלֶךְ הַכָּבוֹד</span><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">מִי הוּא זֶה, &nbsp;&nbsp; מֶלֶךְ הַכָּבוֹד</span><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black;"><br></span><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">יְהוָה צְבָאוֹת-- &nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">הוּא מֶלֶךְ הַכָּבוֹד סֶלָה</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(תהלים פרק כד פסוקים<span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span>ז-י')</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>

<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">34. Recitative-Tenor</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Unto which of the angels said He at any time: "Thou art My Son, this day have I begotten Thee?"</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Hebrews 1: 5</span></strong></p>

<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">ואל המלאכים אמר אתם בניי עדים כי היום ילדתיו</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Hebrews 1: 5)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>

<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 13;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">35. Chorus</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Let all the angels of God worship Him.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Hebrews 1: 6</span></strong></p>

<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">. מקהלה </span></strong><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">35</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">בואו כל מלאכיי לשרת אותו.. </span></p>

<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Hebrews 1: 6)</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 14;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">36. Air- Alto or soprano</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Thou art gone up on high; Thou hast led captivity captive, and received gifts for men; yea, even from Thine enemies, that the Lord</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">God might dwell among them.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Psalm 68: 18</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">36. ארייה- אלט או סופרן </span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">עָלִיתָ לַמָּרוֹם, שָׁבִיתָ שֶּׁבִי- &nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">לָקַחְתָּ מַתָּנוֹת, בָּאָדָם</span><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black;"><br></span><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">וְאַף סוֹרְרִים, &nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">לִשְׁכֹּן יָהּ אֱלֹהִים</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; mso-bidi-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">תהלים פרק סח' פסוק יח')</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: center; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="center"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
