</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 15;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">37. Chorus</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">The Lord gave the word; great was the company of the preachers.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Psalm 68: 11</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">. מקהלה</span></strong><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">37</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">אֲדֹנָי יִתֶּן-אֹמֶר; &nbsp;&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">הַמְבַשְּׂרוֹת, צָבָא רָב</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">תהלים פרק סח' פסוק יא')</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 16;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="margin-right: -17.4pt; text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">38. Air (or « duet and Chorus »)-Soprano or alto (or soprano, alto and Chorus)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">How beautiful are the feet of them that preach the gospel of peace, and bring glad tidings of good things.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Isaiah 52: 7; Romans 10: 15</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">38. מקהלה, או דואט </span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">סופרן או אלט (או סופרן אלט ומקהלה)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">מַה-נָּאווּ עַל-הֶהָרִים רַגְלֵי מְבַשֵּׂר, </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">מַשְׁמִיעַ שָׁלוֹם מְבַשֵּׂר טוֹב--מַשְׁמִיעַ יְשׁוּעָה;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">ישעיה פרק נב פסוק י-יה'</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 17;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">39. Chorus (or air for tenor)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Their sound is gone out into all lands,<br>and their words unto the ends of the world.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Romans 10: 18; Psalm 19: 4</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">39. מקהלה או ארייה טנור</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">בְּכָל-הָאָרֶץ, יָצָא קַוָּם, &nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">וּבִקְצֵה תֵבֵל, מִלֵּיהֶםַ</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">תהלים פרק יח' פסוק ה')</span></strong><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 18;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="margin-right: -15.7pt; text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">40. Air (or « Air and Recitative »)-Bass</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Why do the nations so furiously rage together, and why do the people imagine a vain thing?<br>The kings of the earth rise up, and the rulers take counsel together against the Lord, and against His anointed.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Psalm 2: 1-2)</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; text-indent: -5.95pt; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">40. ארייה או ארייה רצ'יטטיב- בס</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; mso-ascii-font-family: Arial; mso-hansi-font-family: Arial; color: black; background: white;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">לָמָּה, רָגְשׁוּ גוֹיִם; &nbsp;&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>וּלְאֻמִּים, יֶהְגּוּ-רִיק</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">יִתְיַצְּבוּ, מַלְכֵי-אֶרֶץ- &nbsp;&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>וְרוֹזְנִים נוֹסְדוּ-יָחַד</span><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black;"><br></span><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">עַל-יְהוָה, &nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">וְעַל-מְשִׁיחו</span><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; mso-ascii-font-family: Arial; mso-hansi-font-family: Arial; color: black; background: white;">ֹ</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">תהלים פרק ב' פסוק א-ב')</span></strong><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 19;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">41. Chorus</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Let us break their bonds asunder, and cast away their yokes from us.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Psalm 2: 3)</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">41. מקהלה</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">נְנַתְּקָה, אֶת-מוֹסְרוֹתֵימוֹ; &nbsp;&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>וְנַשְׁלִיכָה מִמֶּנּוּ עֲבֹתֵימוֹ</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">תהלים פרק ב' פסוק ג')</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 20;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">42. Recitative- Tenor</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">He that dwelleth in Heav'n shall laugh them to scorn; The Lord shall have them in derision.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Psalm 2: 4)</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">. רצ'יטטיב- טנור</span></strong><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">42</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">יוֹשֵׁב בַּשָּׁמַיִם יִשְׂחָק:</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">אֲדֹנָי, יִלְעַג-לָמוֹ</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(תהלים פרק ב' פסוק ד')</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 21;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">43. Air-Tenor</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Thou shalt break them with a rod of iron; thou shalt dash them in pieces like a potter's vessel</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Psalm 2: 9</span></strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">.</span></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">. ארייה- טנור</span></strong><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">43</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; color: black; background: white;">תְּרֹעֵם, בְּשֵׁבֶט בַּרְזֶל: &nbsp;&nbsp; כִּכְלִי יוֹצֵר תְּנַפְּצֵם</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(תהלים פרק ב' פסוק ט')</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>

</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 22; mso-yfti-lastrow: yes;">
<td style="width: 262.25pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="350">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">44. Chorus</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Hallelujah: for the Lord God Omnipotent reign eth.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Revelation 19: 6</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">The kingdom of this world is become the kingdom of our Lord,<br>and of His Christ; and He shall reign for ever and ever.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Revelation 19: 16</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">King of Kings, and Lord of Lords.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Revelation 19: 16)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;">Hallelujah!</span></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 232.75pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="310">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">44. מקהלה</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">הללויה, מלכותך מלכות כל עולמים.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Revelation 19: 6)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">וממשלתך בכל דור ודור </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">ומשיחו עד עולם.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span></span><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Revelation 19: 16)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">מלך מלכי המלכים ואדון האדונים </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Revelation 19: 16)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #252525;">הללויה!</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
</div>

<div dir="ltr" align="left">

<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 0; mso-yfti-firstrow: yes;">
<td style="width: 261.0pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="348">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">45. Air-Soprano</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">I know that my Redeemer liveth,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>and that He shall stand<br>at the latter day upon the earth.<br>And though worms destroy this body, yet in my flesh shall I see God.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Job 19: 25-26)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">For now is Christ risen from the dead, the first fruits of them that sleep.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">I Corinthians 15: 20</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 234.0pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-left: none; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="312">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">45. ארייה- סופרן</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">אני <span style="color: #252525;">יודע כי הגאולים חיים, </span></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">ושהוא יעמוד אחרון על פני האדמה</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">ולמרות שהתולעים אוכלים בבשרי</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">עדיין אני רואה את אלוהים.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Job 19: 25-26)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">וכעת ישו קם לתחייה מן המתים, </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">ומקיץ את כל הנרדמים. </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(I Corinthians 15: 20)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 1;">
<td style="width: 261.0pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="348">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">46. Chorus</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Since by man came death, by man came also the resurrection of the dead.<br>For as in Adam all die, even so in Christ shall all be made alive.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(I Corinthians 15: 21-22</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 234.0pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="312">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">46. מקהלה</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">מאז על ידי איש בא המוות, </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">על ידי איש תבא גם תחיית המתים</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">כבאדם כל ימות, גם כך במשיח הכל בחיים</span><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(I Corinthians 15: 21-22)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 2;">
<td style="width: 261.0pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="348">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">47. Accompagnato-Bass</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Behold, I tell you a mystery; we shall not all sleep, but we shall all be changed in a moment, in the twinkling of an eye, at the last trumpet.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">I Corinthians 15: 51-52</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 234.0pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="312">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">47. רצ'יטטיב מלווה- בס </span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">הנה, אני אומר לך בגדר תעלומה; לא כולנו ישנים, אבל כולנו נהייה שונים ברגע, בהרף עין, בחצוצרה האחרונה..</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">I Corinthians 15: 51-52</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 3;">
<td style="width: 261.0pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="348">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">48. Air- Bass</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">The trumpet shall sound, and the dead shall be raised incorruptible, and we shall be changed.<br>For this corruptible must put on incorruption and this mortal must put on immortality.<br></span><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">I Corinthians 15: 52-53</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 234.0pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="312">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">48. ארייה- בס</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="RTL"><span lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black; mso-fareast-language: EN-US;">עם תקיעת השופר האחרונה</span><span dir="LTR" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black; mso-fareast-language: EN-US;">.<br></span><span lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black; mso-fareast-language: EN-US;">כי יריע השופר</span><span dir="LTR" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black; mso-fareast-language: EN-US;"><br></span><span lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black; mso-fareast-language: EN-US;">והמתים יקומו לתחייה בלי כיליון</span><span dir="LTR" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black; mso-fareast-language: EN-US;"><br></span><span lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black; mso-fareast-language: EN-US;">ואנחנו נשתנה</span><span dir="LTR" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black; mso-fareast-language: EN-US;">.<br style="mso-special-character: line-break;"><!-- [if !supportLineBreakNewLine]--><br style="mso-special-character: line-break;"><!--[endif]--></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;</span>Corinthians 15: 52-53)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; mso-bidi-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 4;">
<td style="width: 261.0pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="348">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">49. Recitative-Alto</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Then shall be brought to pass the saying that is written: "Death is swallowed up in victory."</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">I Corinthians 15: 54</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 234.0pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="312">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">49. רצ'יטטיב- אלט</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="RTL"><span lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black; mso-fareast-language: EN-US;">אז יתקיים הכתוב</span><span dir="LTR" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black; mso-fareast-language: EN-US;">:<br></span><span lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif;">בִּילָע</span><span lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black; mso-fareast-language: EN-US;"> [נבלע] המוות לנצח [עד עולם]</span><span dir="LTR" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black; mso-fareast-language: EN-US;">.<br></span><span lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black; mso-fareast-language: EN-US;">מוות, איה עוקצך</span><span dir="LTR" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black; mso-fareast-language: EN-US;">?<br></span><span lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt;">שְאוֹל</span><span lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black; mso-fareast-language: EN-US;">, איה ניצחונך</span><span lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt;"><span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">I Corinthians 15: 54)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 5;">
<td style="width: 261.0pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="348">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">50. Duet- Alto &amp; tenor</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">O death, where is thy sting? O grave, where is thy victory?<br>The sting of death is sin, and the strength of sin is the law.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 13.5pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 234.0pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="312">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">. דואט- אלט וטנור</span></strong><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">50</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">או המות, היכן עוקצך? או הקבר, היכן נצחונך?</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">העוקץ של המות הוא החטא,<span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span>וכוחו של החטא הוא החוק.</span><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; mso-ascii-font-family: Arial; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; mso-hansi-font-family: Arial;"> </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(I Corinthians 15: 55-56)</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 6;">
<td style="width: 261.0pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="348">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">51. Chorus</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">But thanks be to God, who giveth us the victory through our Lord Jesus Christ.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">I Corinthians 15: 57)</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 234.0pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="312">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">51. מקהלה</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">אבל תודה לאל, שנתן לנו את הנצחון דרך אדוננו ישוע משיח</span><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(I Corinthians 15: 57)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 7;">
<td style="width: 261.0pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="348">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">52. Air-Soprano alto</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">If God be for us, who can be against us</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Romans 8: 31</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Who shall lay anything to the charge of God's elect? It is God that jus ti fi eth, who is he that condemn eth? It is Christ that died, yea rather, that is risen again, who is</span><strong><span style="font-size: 13.5pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;"> </span></strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">at the right hand of God, who makes intercession for us.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">Romans 8: 33-34</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 234.0pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="312">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">. ארייה- סופרן ואלט</span></strong><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">52</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">אם האלוהים עמנו מי יכול לנו. </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Romans 8: 31</span></strong><span style="font-size: 10.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">)</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">אלוהים שייך לבעלי אמונה</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">זה ישו שמת </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">זה שעלה השמימה </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">זה<span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp; </span>שנמצא לימינו של האל</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">מי שמשתדל עבורנו</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Romans 8: 33-34)</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr style="mso-yfti-irow: 8; mso-yfti-lastrow: yes;">
<td style="width: 261.0pt; border: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top: none; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="348">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">53. Chorus</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">Worthy is the Lamb that was slain, and hath redeemed us to God by His blood, to receive power, and riches, and wisdom,<br>and strength, and hon our, and glory, and blessing.<br>Blessing and hon our, glory and power, be unto Him that sit teth upon the throne, and unto the Lamb, for ever and ever.<br>Amen.</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Revelation 5: 12-14)</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="mso-margin-top-alt: auto; mso-margin-bottom-alt: auto; text-align: left; mso-outline-level: 3; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><strong><span style="font-size: 13.5pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: black;">&nbsp;</span></strong></p>
</td>
<td style="width: 234.0pt; border-top: none; border-left: none; border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-right: solid windowtext 1.0pt; mso-border-top-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-left-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; mso-border-alt: solid windowtext .5pt; padding: 0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;" width="312">
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; background: white; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed; margin: 0cm 0cm 1.2pt 36.0pt;" align="right"><strong><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">. מקהלה</span></strong><strong><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: red;">53</span></strong></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">הראוי השה לעולה, </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">ובדמו גאל אותנו האלוהים</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">לקבל כוח עושר וחוכמה, </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">וכוח וכבוד, ותהילה וברכה,</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">ברכה וכבוד, תהילה וכוח, </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">היושב ליד כיסא הכבוד</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">לעולם ועד..</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><span dir="RTL" lang="HE" style="font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: 'Arial',sans-serif; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">אמן... </span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: left; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;"><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span><span style="mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman';">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" dir="LTR" style="text-align: right; direction: ltr; unicode-bidi: embed;" align="right"><strong><span style="font-size: 9.0pt; mso-fareast-font-family: 'Times New Roman'; color: #c00000;">(Revelation 5: 12-14)</span></strong></p>
</td>
</tr>
